values for caching ----\n\ndef percentile_inc(values, k):\n    vals = sorted(values)\n    n = len(vals)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    if n == 1:\n        return float(vals[0])\n    r = 1 + (n - 1) * k\n    lo = int(math.floor(r))\n    hi = int(math.ceil(r))\n    if lo < 1:\n        return float(vals[0])\n    if hi > n:\n        return float(vals[-1])\n    if lo == hi:\n        return
